TL;DR: 本文针对连续波驱动高品质因子微谐振腔光子对源,揭示了有限时间探测窗口对Hong-Ou-Mandel干涉可见度的关键作用,突破了传统联合谱振幅理论预测的零可见度限制,实现了高达0.992的干涉可见度和接近单位的预报效率,为量子网络提供了可扩展的片上光子源平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络需要可扩展的光子源,要求窄线宽、高效率和高不可分辨性。微谐振腔光子对源具有潜力,但传统基于联合谱振幅(JSA)的理论预测独立连续波驱动微谐振腔产生的光子间Hong-Ou-Mandel干涉可见度接近零,这与实验观测不符。因此需要重新理解探测条件下的量子态特性,以实现高性能的片上量子光源。

Method: 1. 理论方法:结合有限时间探测与腔增强自发四波混频过程,建立探测器条件下的预报态模型,证明预报光子态由闲频光子探测窗口决定,而非仅由JSA决定;2. 优化策略:独立优化闲频光子和信号光子的探测时间窗口,在无需频谱滤波或复杂源工程的情况下,同时实现预报光子不可分辨性和本征预报效率接近单位;3. 实验平台:利用集成高品质因子氮化硅微谐振腔,进行HOM干涉测量验证。

Result: 使用集成高品质因子氮化硅微谐振腔,在无背景扣除条件下实现了两组实验结果:1) HOM干涉可见度0.992(8),四重符合计数率4.5(3) Hz;2) HOM干涉可见度0.942(12),四重符合计数率12.2(6) Hz。结果证明通过优化探测窗口可同时获得接近单位的光子不可分辨性和预报效率。

Conclusion: 本工作突破了传统JSA理论框架的局限,揭示了探测窗口在决定预报光子量子态中的关键作用。证明了连续波驱动的高品质因子微谐振腔无需复杂的频谱工程即可实现高可见度HOM干涉和高预报效率,为量子网络基元提供了鲁棒且可扩展的片上集成平台,推动了实用化量子网络的发展。

Abstract: Quantum networks require scalable photon sources combining narrow linewidth, high efficiency, and high indistinguishability. Microresonator photon-pair sources are promising candidates, yet a source-only description based on the joint spectral amplitude (JSA) predicts near-zero Hong-Ou-Mandel (HOM) interference visibility between photons generated by independent, CW-driven microresonators. In this work, we show that the observed HOM interference is not determined by the JSA alone. By combining finite-time detection with cavity-enhanced spontaneous four-wave mixing, we characterize a detector-conditioned heralded state governed by the idler-photon detection window. We further demonstrate that independently optimizing the idler and signal detection windows allows both heralded-photon indistinguishability and intrinsic heralding efficiency to approach unity, without spectral filtering or complex source engineering. Utilizing integrated high-$Q$ silicon nitride microresonators, we achieve HOM visibilities of 0.992(8) and 0.942(12), without background subtraction, at fourfold count rates of 4.5(3) and 12.2(6) Hz, respectively. Our work establishes CW-driven high-$Q$ microresonators as a robust and scalable platform for quantum-network primitives.

</details>


### [100] [From Reversible Quantum Dynamics to Statistical Probability: A dynamical solution to the origin of probability and Hilbert's sixth problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18593)
*Wei-Min Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于精确开放量子系统理论,构建了从可逆量子动力学到约化统计的动力学路径,证明了概率是复合系统中开放系统的客观统计结构,而非外加的随机假设,为希尔伯特第六问题中"从确定性到统计性"提供了精确可检验的动力学解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希尔伯特第六问题的核心挑战是如何从可逆的、确定性的系统动力学产生不可逆的统计概率和热力学。传统理论中,概率通常被视为附加在幺正动力学之上的随机假设。本研究旨在通过精确的开放量子系统理论,揭示概率的客观统计起源,解决从确定性动力学到统计行为的根本问题。

Method: 研究采用精确开放量子系统理论框架,针对与任意环境双线性耦合的二次玻色子和费米子系统,推导出由耗散传播子u和涨落关联函数v完全决定的精确约化密度算符。从系统和环境的纯态乘积出发(移除通常假设的初始热混合环境),通过幺正演化分析系统-环境纠缠的产生过程。研究了连续谱密度(无束缚态极点)和具有局域束缚态的能隙谱密度两种情况,并考察环境自由度数趋于无穷的极限行为。

Result: 对于连续谱密度(无束缚态极点),耗散传播子u在长时间极限下消失,系统初态信息完全丢失,约化密度算符趋向热吉布斯态。具有局域束缚态的能隙谱密度则保持长时记忆,阻止热化。对有限环境,幺正演化首先产生系统-环境纠缠和周期性准吉布斯约化态;当环境自由度趋于无穷时,谱变为连续,回归消失,精确约化密度算符收敛到热吉布斯态。总熵保持为零,而系统熵成为纠缠熵并达到最大值。

Conclusion: 概率不是施加在幺正动力学上的额外随机假设,而是复合系统中开放系统的客观统计结构。研究为从确定性到统计性的转变提供了精确、可检验的动力学解决方案,回应了希尔伯特第六问题关于如何从可逆确定性动力学导出不可逆统计概率和热力学的核心诉求,揭示了量子纠缠在统计概率起源中的根本作用。

Abstract: Hilbert's sixth problem placed probability and mechanics at the center of the axiomatization of physics. It asks how irreversible statistical probability and thermodynamics can produce from the reversible, deterministic dynamics of a system. Building on an exact open quantum system theory developed in the past two decades, we construct a dynamical route from the reversible quantum dynamics to reduced statistics. For quadratic bosonic and fermionic systems bilinearly coupled to arbitrary environments, we derive the exact reduced density operator determined completely by a dissipative propagator u and a fluctuation correlation function v of the system. For continuous spectral densities with no bound-state pole, u vanishes in the long-time limit, information about the system initial states is lost completely, and the reduced density operator of systems approaches a thermal Gibbs state. Gapped spectral densities with localized bound states instead preserve long-time memory and prevent thermalization. We then remove the usual assumption of an initially thermal, mixed environment, start from a product of pure states for the system and environment, unitary evolution first generates system-environment entanglement and a periodic quasi-Gibbs reduced state for a finite environment. When the number of environmental degrees of freedom tends to infinity, the spectrum becomes continuous, the recurrence disappears, the exact reduced density operator converges to a thermal Gibbs state. The total entropy remains zero, whereas the system entropy becomes entanglement entropy and reaches its maximum. Probability is therefore not an additional random postulate imposed on unitary dynamics, it is the objective statistical structure of an open system in a composite. These results provide an exact, testable dynamical solution of the determinism to statistics.

</details>


### [101] [Resonance-Protected Pointer States: Stationary-Phase Analysis of Entanglement in an Environment-Coupled Two-Spin System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18641)
*Kentaro Urasaki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了两个自旋-1/2系统在静态环境场和相互耦合作用下的退相干动力学,发现在共振条件下,最大纠缠态之间的相干以异常缓慢的t^(-1/2)幂律衰减,揭示了指针基不由局域耦合决定而由两环境间统计共振条件决定的新机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索两个量子系统同时受环境退相干和相互耦合影响时的动力学行为,特别是研究非局域耦合机制如何影响指针基的选择,挑战传统的基于局域系统-环境耦合的指针态理论(Zurek模型)。

Method: 构建精确解利用宇称对称性;在环境场连续极限下应用稳相近似(鞍点方法);分析孤立驻点和共振条件下的相干衰减行为;与Zurek指针基理论(1981和2005模型)进行对比分析,识别系统中的非局域机制。

Result: 普通孤立驻点相关的相干以t^(-1)幂律衰减;在共振条件下(两自旋局域有效场相互抵消时),只有最大纠缠Bell态对(系统间耦合的本征基)之间的相干存活,并以异常缓慢的t^(-1/2)幂律衰减;指针可观测量由两环境间的统计共振条件决定,而非单独的系统-环境耦合。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了量子退相干中一种不可约化为局域耦合的新机制:在具有相互耦合的双系统中,指针基的选择由两个环境之间的统计共振条件决定,而非传统理论所强调的局域系统-环境相互作用。共振条件下的最大纠缠态展现出显著增强的退相干保护(t^(-1/2)衰减率),为理解多体量子系统中的退相干和纠缠保护提供了新视角。

Abstract: In this manuscript, we analyze the dynamics of a model in which two spin-1/2 systems are each coupled noncommutingly to a static environmental field and to a self-Hamiltonian, while also being mutually coupled through a $σ_y^{(1)}σ_y^{(2)}$ interaction. By constructing an exact solution that exploits parity symmetry, and by applying the stationary-phase approximation (the saddle-point method) in the continuum limit of the environmental field, we show that coherence associated with the ordinary isolated stationary point decays as $t^{-1}$, whereas, under the resonance condition at which the local effective fields of the two spins cancel each other, only the coherence between a pair of maximally entangled, Bell-type states- the eigenbasis of the inter-system coupling-survives, decaying via the anomalously slow power law $t^{-1/2}$. Through a comparison with the pointer-basis theory of W. H. Zurek and coworkers (the 1981 and 2005 models), we further reveal a mechanism not reducible to local coupling: in this system, the pointer observable is determined not by the individual system-environment couplings but by a statistical resonance condition between the two environments.

</details>


### [102] [Impact of a CSS quantum error correction code in underwater quantum key distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18666)
*Juliette Florin,Nicolas Le Josse,Arnaud Coatanhay,Gilles Burel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了两种四量子比特CSS量子纠错码在水下光学信道BB84量子密钥分发协议中的应用,通过建立包含光子损耗、几何扩散和太阳噪声的随机信道模型,分析了量子比特错误率和安全密钥率。研究表明,量子纠错码仅在边缘信噪比环境下有益,可在牺牲吞吐量的代价下扩展通信范围,而在高信噪比下原始BB84协议更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下光学信道中的显著光子损耗(擦除)和环境噪声严重降低了量子密钥分发(QKD)的性能,影响海事基础设施所需的安全水下通信。本文旨在探究量子纠错码(QECC)能否有效缓解这些损伤,特别是在垂直水下通信场景中应用BB84 QKD协议时的性能改善潜力。

Method: 建立了综合随机信道模型,包含光子损耗、几何扩散和太阳噪声等因素;采用两种四量子比特Calderbank-Shor-Steane(CSS)量子纠错码(标准CSS码和丢弃码变体);通过解析研究量子比特错误率(QBER)和安全密钥率(SKR)随信噪比(SNR)的变化关系,包括有安全性和无安全性两种情况;使用蒙特卡洛模拟验证分析结果;针对Jerlov III型海水和大气顶层太阳光照条件进行场景分析。

Result: 标准四量子比特CSS码在QBER 11%安全阈值处实现3 dB信噪比增益;丢弃码变体实现4.5 dB增益;量子纠错码的编码开销会降低安全密钥率;揭示了安全密钥率与发送光子到达概率之间的关键关系;证明量子纠错码仅在边缘信噪比环境下有益,而在高信噪比下原始BB84协议占优;针对Jerlov III型海水场景,识别出量子纠错码能够实现原本不可行通信的特定深度-距离操作窗口。

Conclusion: 量子纠错码在水下量子密钥分发中的部署必须依据具体场景决策:在边缘信噪比条件下,以牺牲吞吐量为代价扩展操作范围;在高信噪比条件下,优先考虑密钥生成速率而采用原始BB84协议。纠错码的应用不是普适解决方案,而是需要根据信噪比条件、通信深度和距离等参数进行权衡优化的场景依赖性技术。

Abstract: Quantum key distribution (QKD) enables secure underwater communications essential for maritime infrastructure. Underwater optical channels introduce substantial photon loss (erasures) and ambient noise that degrade QKD performance. This paper investigates whether two four-qubit Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS) quantum error correction codes (QECC) mitigate these impairments in vertical underwater communication BB84 QKD protocol. After developing a comprehensive stochastic channel model incorporating photon loss, geometric spreading, and solar noise, we assess the viability of QECC through the analytical study of the quantum bit error rate (QBER) and the secure key rate (SKR) with and without security depending on the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) validated against Monte Carlo simulations. The standard four-qubit CSS code achieves a 3 dB SNR gain at the QBER 11% security threshold; the discard code variant achieves 4.5 dB. However, QECC includes an encoding overhead that reduces the SKR. We demonstrate a crucial relationship between the SKR and the probability of arrival of the sent photon. Analysis shows that QECC is beneficial exclusively in marginal SNR regimes; at high SNR, raw BB84 dominates. For an ocean Type III Jerlov water scenario with sunlight coming from the sun located at the top of the atmosphere, we identify operational depth-range windows where QECC enables communication otherwise infeasible. This analysis establishes that error correction deployment must be scenario-dependent: extend operational range at the cost of throughput when SNR is marginal, or prioritize key generation rates at high SNR.

</details>


### [103] [Theory-agnostic nonclassicality certification in an integrated photonic circuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18671)
*Vinicius P. Rossi,Emanuele Polino,Beatrice Polacchi,Valeria Cimini,David Schmid,John H. Selby,Giacomo Corrielli,Andrea Crespi,Roberto Osellame,Fabio Sciarrino,Ana Belén Sainz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报告了在三模集成光子电路上实现的理论无关广义情境性认证，通过理论无关层析成像与单纯形可嵌入性认证相结合的方法，在不依赖特定物理理论假设的情况下，证明了光子电路展现非经典性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认证非经典性而不假设任何特定物理理论既是基础性挑战，也是设备无关协议的要求。在可扩展架构中实现这种理论无关认证对于连接基础测试与新兴量子技术至关重要。集成光子电路是可扩展量子信息处理的主流平台，因此需要开发严格方法来认证支撑量子优势的非经典资源。

Method: 采用理论无关层析成像(theory-agnostic tomography)与单纯形可嵌入性认证(simplex-embeddability certification)相结合的框架，该方法不需要对底层物理理论做任何假设。将该方法应用于由单光子激发的三模光子电路实验数据。独立构建的实验量子模型用于对观测到的非经典性进行一致性检验，但不作为理论假设纳入认证过程。

Result: 该方法排除了实验数据的单纯形可嵌入性，为光子电路展现非经典性提供了稳健的、理论无关的证据。实验在三模集成光子电路上成功实现了广义情境性的理论无关认证。

Conclusion: 本研究在集成光子电路这一可扩展量子平台上实现了广义情境性的理论无关认证，证明了光子电路的非经典性质。该工作为设备无关量子协议提供了严格的认证方法，建立了基础量子测试与实际量子技术应用之间的桥梁，对推动可扩展量子信息处理具有重要意义。

Abstract: Certifying nonclassicality without assuming any particular underlying physical theory constitutes both a foundational challenge and a requirement for device-independent protocols. Realizing such theory-independent certification in scalable architectures is essential for connecting fundamental tests with emerging quantum technologies. Integrated photonic circuits are a leading platform for scalable quantum information processing, motivating the development of rigorous methods to certify the nonclassical resources underpinning quantum advantage. In this paper, we report a theory-independent certification of generalized contextuality on a three-mode integrated photonic circuit. Our approach combines theory-agnostic tomography with simplex-embeddability certification---a framework that requires no assumptions about the underlying physical theory---and applies it to experimental data from a three-mode photonic circuit seeded by single photons. An independently constructed quantum model of the experiment provides a consistency check on the observed nonclassicality, without entering the certification as a theoretical assumption. The method rules out simplex-embeddability for our experimental data, providing robust, theory-independent evidence that our photonic circuit exhibits nonclassicality.

</details>


### [104] [Universal entanglement embezzlement and divergent nonlocal magic from generic local chaotic quantum evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18691)
*Matias Karjula,Teemu Ojanen,Kim Pöyhönen,Tapio Ala-Nissila,Moein N. Ivaki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究表明局域幺正量子演化从乘积态出发,可在中间阶段自然生成具有多尺度纠缠谱结构的态,这些态能够实现通用纠缠embezzlement(挪用),即从催化剂多体态中提取纠缠而使其保持渐近不变。这些非典型结构在达到最大熵之前就普遍出现,并伴随随系统尺寸发散的非局域非稳定性,证明混沌量子演化无需精细调控即可生成通用催化储库态族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为具有通用纠缠embezzlement能力的催化剂态需要特殊构造。本研究动机是探索这类特殊的多尺度纠缠谱结构是否能在自然量子演化过程中自发涌现,特别是在系统达到热化平衡之前的中间阶段,从而为催化纠缠操作提供更普适的物理基础。

Method: 从乘积态初态出发,通过局域幺正算符进行量子演化;分析演化过程中中间态的纠缠谱结构特征;考察这些中间态是否满足通用纠缠embezzlement所需的多尺度谱层级结构;测量伴随的非局域非稳定性(nonstabilizerness)及其随系统尺寸的标度行为;验证其与近期建立的通用embezzlement与发散非局域magic等价性的一致性。

Result: 局域幺正量子演化在中间阶段普遍生成具有多尺度纠缠谱结构的态,这些态支持通用纠缠embezzlement;这些非典型结构的出现早于系统达到最大熵的热化阶段;伴随的非局域非稳定性随系统尺寸发散增长;观测结果与通用embezzlement和发散非局域magic之间的等价关系相符;混沌量子演化无需任何精细调控即可产生这类特殊态。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了一个重要的普适性结果:混沌量子演化过程自然产生的中间态构成了通用催化储库态族,无需人为精细调控。这些态在热化完成前就已具备通用纠缠embezzlement所需的多尺度谱结构和发散的非局域magic特性。这一发现为量子信息处理中的催化纠缠操作提供了自然的物理实现途径,深化了对量子热化过程中纠缠结构演化的理解。

Abstract: We show that, starting from a product state, local unitary quantum evolutions generate intermediate states which exhibit a multiscale entanglement-spectrum structure required for universal entanglement embezzlement. This facilitates entanglement extraction from a catalyst many-body state while leaving it asymptotically unchanged. Remarkably, these atypical structures emerge generically at intermediate stages, well before reaching maximum entropy where thermalization has flattened out the spectral hierarchy. The resulting state is accompanied by nonlocal nonstabilizerness that diverges with the system size, consistent with a recently established equivalence between universal embezzlement and divergent nonlocal magic. Thus, without any fine tuning, a chaotic quantum evolution generates intermediate states which form a universal family of catalytic reservoirs.

</details>


### [105] [Thermal entanglement on a frustrated tetrahedron: Probing quantum resources through concurrence and stabilizer structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18721)
*Reza Pourkhodabakhshi,Francis Dominie,Deep Gajera,Stephanie H. Curnoe*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了四面体上四个自旋-1/2磁矩系统的热态量子纠缠性质，通过线性优化方法确定多体并发纠缠的上界，发现在受阻自旋系统中高度纠缠的本征态可以形成可分离的热混合态，并识别出不同耦合区域在有限温度下的纠缠特征和量子魔力分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在受阻自旋系统中，高度纠缠的本征态可能形成可分离的热混合态。为了理解这种本征态纠缠与热态纠缠之间的差异，需要系统研究四面体自旋系统（烧绿石晶格的基本单元）在不同温度和耦合参数下的热纠缠性质，并为超越基态限制的量子资源研究提供实用的理论基础。

Method: 利用四面体对称性约束，通过线性优化方法搜索热态分解，计算多体并发纠缠(multipartite concurrence)的上界；在上界消失处给出显式可分离分解；使用负性(negativity)独立验证选定耦合区域的有限温度纠缠；采用混合态稳定子Rényi诊断方法探索量子魔力(quantum magic)的分布特征；分析对称多重态的分裂和布居如何影响热纠缠。

Result: 在低温下，并发纠缠图显示在全进全出(all-in-all-out)极限附近存在扩展的可分离区域，而在自旋冰点附近的抑制区域则狭窄局域；加热会使后者区域变宽，因为附近的多重态被重新布居；即使本征态平均并发纠缠保持较大值，优化方法仍能识别出完全可分离的热态；负性测量在选定耦合区域证实了有限温度纠缠的存在；量子魔力的混合态稳定子Rényi诊断倾向于不同的耦合参数，并可能随温度局部增加。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了热纠缠与对称多重态分裂和布居之间的联系，表明高度纠缠的本征态可以在有限温度下形成可分离的热混合态。线性优化方法为识别受阻自旋构型在超越基态限制的量子资源研究中提供了实用基础。不同的量子资源指标（纠缠和量子魔力）在参数空间和温度依赖性上表现出不同的行为特征，为理解受阻自旋系统中的量子特性提供了全面视角。

Abstract: In a frustrated spin system, highly entangled eigenstates can form a separable thermal mixture. We study this distinction for four spin-$1/2$ moments on a tetrahedron, the elementary unit of the pyrochlore lattice, with the general four-parameter exchange Hamiltonian. Tetrahedral symmetry allows a restricted search over thermal-state decompositions to be carried out by linear optimization, yielding an upper bound on multipartite concurrence and explicit separable decompositions where this bound vanishes. At low temperature, the concurrence maps show extended separable regions near the all-in--all-out limit, whereas suppression near the spin-ice point is narrowly localized. Heating broadens the latter region as nearby multiplets are repopulated. The optimization identifies fully separable thermal states even when the eigenstate-averaged concurrence remains large. Negativity independently confirms finite-temperature entanglement in selected coupling regions. A mixed-state stabilizer Rényi diagnostic, used to explore quantum magic, favours different couplings and can increase locally with temperature. These results connect thermal entanglement to the splitting and population of symmetry multiplets, and provide a practical basis for identifying frustrated spin configurations for studies of quantum resources beyond the ground-state limit.

</details>


### [106] [All coherent measurements provide observational ergotropic advantage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18735)
*Soumik Mahanti,Rakesh Saini,Alexei Gilchrist,Arindam Mitra*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了测量相干性与量子系统功提取优势之间的定量关系,提出了一个忠实的操作量化指标,证明了测量相干性是获得正优势的充要条件,并从资源理论角度揭示了测量相干性的热力学价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究确立了测量相干性对于从未知孤立量子系统中提取功获得优势的必要性,但缺乏这种优势的大小与已建立的测量相干性度量之间的定量关系。需要建立测量相干性与功提取优势之间明确的数学联系,以深入理解测量相干性作为量子资源的热力学意义。

Method: 研究引入了一个忠实的操作量化指标来刻画测量提供的功提取优势。利用测量相干性的鲁棒性(robustness)和基于$l_{\infty}$范数的相干性度量,分别推导出功提取优势的上界和下界。从资源理论的视角系统性地分析了这一优势量化指标,建立了测量相干性与功提取优势之间的充要条件和定量边界。

Result: 证明了测量相干性是获得正的功提取优势的充要条件。成功推导出功提取优势的上界(由测量相干性鲁棒性给出)和下界(由$l_{\infty}$范数相干性度量给出)。建立了测量相干性资源含量与其热力学价值之间的非平凡关系,为测量相干性提供了操作性刻画。

Conclusion: 本研究为测量相干性作为功提取资源提供了操作性刻画,建立了测量相干性度量与功提取优势之间的定量联系。结果揭示了量子测量的相干性资源含量与其在热力学工作提取中的实际价值之间存在深刻的内在联系,丰富了量子热力学和量子资源理论的交叉研究,为利用量子相干性优化能量提取过程提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The use of quantum resources in the work extraction from a quantum system is an emerging research topic. Recently, [arXiv:2602.22893] established the necessity of measurement coherence for obtaining advantage in work extraction from an unknown isolated quantum system. However, a quantitative relation between the magnitude of this advantage and established measures of measurement coherence is missing. Here, we establish such a connection by introducing a faithful operational quantifier of the work advantage provided by a measurement. We show that measurement coherence is necessary and sufficient for a positive advantage, and derive upper and lower bounds in terms of the robustness of measurement coherence and an $l_{\infty}$ norm based coherence measure respectively. Finally, we examine this quantifier of advantage from the resource theoretic perspective. Our results provide an operational characterisation of measurement coherence as a resource for work extraction and reveal a nontrivial relation between its resource content and thermodynamic value.

</details>


### [107] [Almost One Bit Violation of Minimum-Output Rényi Entropy Additivity Simultaneously at All Orders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18747)
*Guocheng Zhen,Chengkai Zhu,Ranyiliu Chen,Xin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了最小输出Rényi熵的可加性在所有非负阶数上可以同时失效近1比特，构造了一个有限维量子信道，其张量平方在所有p∈[0,∞]上都存在至少log2-ε的熵间隙，输出维度为O(ε^(-3))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信息理论中的Rényi熵可加性问题是一个基本的开放问题。现有研究未能在所有阶数上同时证明可加性的失效，本文旨在构造一个反例，证明最小输出Rényi熵的可加性在所有非负阶数上可以同时大幅失效，从而深化对量子信道输出熵结构的理解。

Method: 本文采用自由群直积的数学结构进行构造：(1)使用张量化的Haagerup估计来控制单拷贝输出；(2)利用不同因子之间的交换性强制在两拷贝情况下产生精确的Bell分支碰撞；(3)通过强收敛性给出两种实现方式：一是通过直角Artin群的有限维表示后进行实化的存在性实现，二是构造Haar正交模型，其成功概率随矩阵维度增长趋于1；(4)确定精确的Bell商，证明渐近最优的正则半径界，并证明立方输出维度尺度在当前纯度-秩证明框架下是最优的。

Result: 对于任意ε∈(0,log2)，构造出一个具有实Stinespring等距的有限维量子信道，同一个最大纠缠输入在所有p∈[0,∞]上见证了至少log2-ε的张量平方熵间隙。输出维度可以选择为O(ε^(-3))当ε趋于0时。证明了精确的Bell商，获得了渐近最优的正则半径界，并确认立方输出维度尺度在现有证明技术下是最优的。

Conclusion: 本文成功证明了最小输出Rényi熵的可加性可以在所有非负阶数上同时失效近1比特，这是量子信息理论中的一个重要反例。通过自由群直积和Haagerup估计的创新组合，构造出具有实Stinespring等距的量子信道，输出维度尺度为O(ε^(-3))。该结果揭示了量子信道输出熵的非可加性本质，为理解量子纠缠和量子信道容量提供了新的数学工具和理论洞察，同时证明了构造方法在技术层面的最优性。

Abstract: We prove that minimum-output Rényi-entropy additivity can fail by almost one bit simultaneously at every nonnegative order. For every $\varepsilon\in(0,\log2)$, there exists a finite-dimensional quantum channel with a real Stinespring isometry such that the same maximally entangled input witnesses a tensor-square entropy gap of at least $\log2-\varepsilon$ for all $p\in[0,\infty]$. The output dimension can be chosen to be $O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ as $\varepsilon\downarrow0$.
  The construction uses direct products of free groups: tensorized Haagerup estimates control the one-copy outputs, while commutation between distinct factors forces exact Bell-branch collisions at two copies. Strong convergence gives both an existential realization through finite-dimensional representations of right-angled Artin groups followed by realification, and a Haar-orthogonal model whose success probability tends to one as the matrix dimension grows. We also determine the exact Bell quotient, prove asymptotically sharp regular-radius bounds, and show that the cubic output-dimension scale is optimal within the present purity--rank certificate.

</details>


### [108] [Query-Optimal and Gate-Efficient Lindbladian Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18757)
*Boyang Chen,Minbo Gao,Xinzhao Wang,Shuo Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量子算法用于Lindblad演化模拟,在给定哈密顿量分块编码和跳跃算符投影酉编码的条件下,该算法达到了与哈密顿量模拟相匹配的查询下界,并实现了近乎最优的查询复杂度和门复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的量子开放系统Lindblad演化模拟算法在查询复杂度和门复杂度方面尚未达到理论最优界限。需要开发一种高效的量子算法,能够在接近哈密顿量模拟查询下界的条件下实现Lindblad方程的精确模拟,同时保持较低的门复杂度。

Method: 算法采用单查询转换器(one-query transducer)结合催化态实现短时演化的有理逼近乘积。使用压缩的Kraus标签表示法,仅存储非零标签的位置和数值,并结合Chen等人的旋转分解技术进行门实现。通过利用不同Kraus标签序列之间的正交性来界定省略催化态产生的误差。算法接受哈密顿量H的分块编码和堆叠跳跃算符B的投影酉编码作为输入,归一化因子分别为α_H和α_B。

Result: 对于演化时间t,设定τ=(α_H+α_B²)t,算法使用O(τ+log(1/ε)/log(e+log(1/ε)/τ))次预言机查询即可将演化信道逼近至菱形范数误差ε,与哈密顿量模拟的查询下界相匹配。额外的单量子比特门和双量子比特门数量在多对数因子范围内与查询复杂度呈线性关系。在相干时间索引预言机访问条件下,查询和门复杂度界可扩展至Lipschitz连续的含时Lindblad演化。

Conclusion: 本文成功构造了一种查询最优的Lindblad演化量子模拟算法,其查询复杂度达到了哈密顿量模拟的理论下界,同时保持了接近线性的门复杂度。该方法通过创新的催化态技术和压缩Kraus标签表示,有效解决了开放量子系统模拟中的效率问题,并可推广至含时Lindblad方程,为量子开放系统模拟提供了理论最优的解决方案。

Abstract: We give a quantum algorithm for Lindbladian simulation given a block encoding of the Hamiltonian $H$ and a projected unitary encoding of the stacked jump operator $B=\sum_{k=1}^m \lvert k\rangle\otimes L_k$, with normalization factors $α_H$ and $α_B$, respectively. For evolution time $t$, set $τ=(α_H+α_B^2)t$. The algorithm approximates the evolution channel to diamond-norm error $\varepsilon$ using $O\!\left(τ+\frac{\log(1/\varepsilon)}{\log\!\left(e+\log(1/\varepsilon)/τ\right)}\right)$ oracle queries, matching the query lower bound for Hamiltonian simulation. The number of additional one- and two-qubit gates is linear in the query complexity up to polylogarithmic factors. The query- and gate-complexity bounds extend to Lipschitz-continuous time-dependent Lindbladians under coherent time-indexed oracle access. Our construction uses a one-query transducer that implements a product of rational approximations to short-time evolution when supplied with a catalyst. We bound the error from omitting the catalyst by exploiting orthogonality between different sequences of Kraus labels. The gate implementation combines a compressed Kraus-label representation, which stores only the positions and values of the nonzero labels, with the rotation factorization of Chen et al.

</details>


### [109] [Feasibility Ordering of Entanglement-Source Placement for Qubit Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18803)
*Samuel Marquez Gonzalez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了在量子比特信道中,纠缠源的中点放置相对于端点放置在可行性意义上总是最优的。通过转置分解准则、量子Sinkhorn缩放和退极化正则化等数学工具,证明了对于任意量子比特信道,如果顺序组合能保持纠缠,则相应的并行作用不能完全消灭纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究基于解析和数值方法提出纠缠源的中点放置应至少与端点放置一样有利,但这一猜想尚未得到严格数学证明。本文旨在为任意量子比特信道严格证明这一最优性猜想,解决纠缠源在噪声量子通信线路中的最优放置问题。

Method: 采用转置分解准则作为核心工具;利用量子Sinkhorn缩放将严格正的量子比特信道转换为幺正表示;应用幺正量子比特信道的特殊标准形式获得转置映射通过原信道的分解;通过退极化正则化和纠缠破缺信道集的闭性将结果推广到任意信道;建立了并行作用(⊗)和顺序组合(∘)之间关于纠缠保持/破缺性质的逻辑关系。

Result: 证明了对于任意量子比特信道,若并行作用Λ₁⊗Λ₂属于纠缠消灭类(EA),则顺序组合Λ₂∘Λ₁必属于纠缠破缺类(EB);通过交换两个信道,反向组合顺序同样成立。这一结果严格证明了中点放置相对于端点放置的优越性:如果顺序组合能保持纠缠,则并行作用必然也能保持部分纠缠。

Conclusion: 本文在原始定义的可行性意义上严格证明了纠缠源中点放置对所有量子比特信道的最优性猜想。该结果为量子通信网络中纠缠源的配置提供了理论保证,表明在两个噪声量子比特信道形成的通信线路上,将纠缠源放置在中点位置总是最优选择,这对量子中继和量子网络的实际设计具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: This work studies the placement of an entanglement source along a communication line formed by two noisy qubit channels. Recent work argued, on analytical and numerical grounds, that midpoint placement should be at least as favorable as endpoint placement. Here it is shown that, for arbitrary qubit channels, if a sequential composition can preserve entanglement, then the corresponding parallel action cannot annihilate all entanglement. The proof uses the transpose-factorization criterion introduced in that recent work. Quantum Sinkhorn scaling converts every strictly positive qubit channel into a unital representative, and the special normal form of unital qubit channels then yields the required factorization of the transposed map through the original channel. Depolarizing regularization and the closedness of the set of entanglement-breaking channels extend the result to arbitrary channels. Consequently, $Λ_1 \otimes Λ_2 \in \mathrm{EA}$ implies $Λ_2 \circ Λ_1 \in \mathrm{EB}$, and, by exchanging the two channels, the same holds for the opposite composition order. This proves the recent conjecture that midpoint placement is optimal for all qubit channels, in the feasibility sense in which that optimality was originally defined.

</details>


### [110] [Fast Evaluation of the Sixth-Order Time-Convolutionless Master-Equation Generator and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18806)
*Jiahao Chen,Sirui Chen,Dragomir Davidovic*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对时间卷积less(TCL)方法在开放量子系统模拟中的计算复杂度问题,推导出精确的算法优化方案。通过分离算符系数与浴核函数、引入递归结构和快速卷积技术,将六阶TCL(TCL6)的计算复杂度从O(N_t^3)降至O(N_t log^2 N_t),并证明了更高阶TCL方法的复杂度可进一步优化,为非马尔可夫开放量子系统的长时模拟提供了高效算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接数值求解Hadamard约化的六阶时间卷积less(TCL6)生成器在所有N_t个采样时间点需要O(N_t^3)的运算量,这在固定系统维度下计算成本过高,严重限制了非马尔可夫开放量子系统的长时间动力学模拟。因此需要开发更高效的算法来降低TCL方法的计算复杂度,使其能够应用于实际的长时演化问题。

Method: 针对通过一个厄米算符耦合到平稳中心高斯浴的有限维系统,推导出精确的算法约化方案:1)将固定的算符系数与标量浴核函数分离;2)将TCL6时间序列约化为累积和与一阶递推关系;3)采用一维因果卷积技术;4)对交错历史引入精确的二元递归算法。此外,证明了TCL2n阶方法可以实现O(N_t log^{n-1} N_t)的复杂度,并且在使用固定有限Matsubara展开表示浴关联函数时,任意固定TCL阶数均可达到O(N_t log N_t)的复杂度。

Result: 成功将完整TCL6时间序列的计算复杂度从O(N_t^3)降低到O(N_t log^2 N_t)(固定系统维度)。证明了一般TCL2n阶方法的复杂度为O(N_t log^{n-1} N_t),并且在采用固定有限Matsubara展开时,任意固定TCL阶数的复杂度可达O(N_t log N_t)。这些复杂度降低使得在TCL展开的有效性范围内进行非马尔可夫开放量子系统的快速长时模拟成为可能。

Conclusion: 本文通过巧妙的数学约化和算法设计,显著降低了时间卷积less方法的计算复杂度,从立方级别优化至准线性级别。这一算法突破为非马尔可夫开放量子系统的长时动力学模拟提供了实用工具,扩展了TCL方法在复杂量子系统研究中的应用范围,特别是在需要长时演化的物理问题中具有重要价值。

Abstract: Direct quadrature of the Hadamard-reduced sixth-order time-convolutionless (TCL6) generator at all $N_t$ sampled times requires $O(N_t^3)$ operations at fixed system dimension. We derive an exact reduction for a finite-dimensional system coupled through one Hermitian operator to a stationary centered Gaussian bath. By separating fixed operator coefficients from scalar bath kernels, the complete TCL6 time series is reduced to cumulative sums and first-order recurrences, one-dimensional causal convolutions, and an exact dyadic recursion for interlocked histories. With the algorithm, evaluating the complete TCL6 time series requires $O(N_t\log^2 N_t)$ operations at fixed system dimension. In addition, we show that TCL$2n$ can be evaluated with $O(N_t\log^{n-1} N_t)$ complexity. A fixed finite Matsubara expansion of the bath correlation function permits $O(N_t\log N_t)$ evaluation at any fixed TCL order. These reductions enable fast long-time simulations of non-Markovian open quantum systems within the regime of validity of the TCL expansion.

</details>


### [111] [Quantum Behaviors Are Not Semialgebraic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18865)
*Minbo Gao,Zhengfeng Ji,Chenghua Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了量子贝尔场景中的量子行为集合是非半代数的,否定了Tsirelson在1993年提出的开放性问题,并表明该集合不存在有限的半定规划表示和精确的层级刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tsirelson在1993年提出了一个长期未解决的基础问题:贝尔场景中通过共享量子态的局域测量所能实现的条件概率集合(量子行为集)是否为半代数集,即是否可以用有限的多项式方程和不等式的布尔组合来描述。回答这一问题对于理解量子关联的数学结构、半定规划表示的可行性以及NPA层级的收敛性具有重要意义。

Method: 研究采用了实解析几何和算子理论的方法,具体分析了每方具有四个二值测量的贝尔场景。通过构造特定的经典行为点,证明了在该点附近,有限维量子行为集、其闭包以及交换算子集都不具有有限实解析描述。利用反证法和局部分析技术,排除了这些集合可能的半代数结构。

Result: 证明了在每方具有四个二值测量的配置下,有限维量子行为集、其闭包以及交换算子集都是非半代数的。更强的结果表明,这些集合即使在特定经典行为点的局部邻域内也不存在有限实解析描述。这些发现排除了精确有限半定规划表示的可能性,并证明Navascués-Pironio-Acín(NPA)层级的任何有限层都无法精确刻画这些集合。

Conclusion: 本文给出了Tsirelson 1993年问题的否定答案,证明量子行为集是非半代数的。这一结果具有深远影响:它表明量子关联的数学结构比此前预期的更为复杂,不存在有限的精确代数刻画;NPA层级虽然在实践中有效,但无法在有限层达到精确收敛;量子非局域性的完整刻画需要超越半代数方法的新数学工具。这为量子信息理论和算子代数的进一步研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: The conditional probabilities achievable by local measurements on a shared quantum state in a Bell scenario form the set of quantum behaviors, whose structure was studied by Tsirelson. In 1993, Tsirelson asked whether this set is semialgebraic, that is, describable by finite Boolean combinations of polynomial equations and inequalities. This question has remained open. We answer it in the negative: with four binary measurements per party, the set of finite-dimensional quantum behaviors, its closure, and the commuting-operator set are all nonsemialgebraic. More strongly, none admits a finite real-analytic description even locally near a particular classical behavior. These results rule out exact finite semidefinite representations and show that no finite level of the Navascués-Pironio-Acín hierarchy characterizes these sets exactly.

</details>


### [112] [Liouvillian exceptional points in the emergence of quantum synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18867)
*Carlos Ortega Taberner,Rosalind E. Cotton,Paul R. Eastham*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了耦合激光器或凝聚体的同步转变的量子理论，揭示了经典极限下的稳态如何对应于Liouvillian慢模式的不同谱结构，并证明同步转变由受广义PT对称性保护的例外点级联控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典或半经典理论中，耦合自持振荡器（如激光器或极化激元凝聚体）在不同耦合强度下会出现不同稳态，从弱耦合的独立振荡到强耦合的同步化。这些稳态转变在经典理论中对应于相空间吸引子拓扑结构的改变，发生在广义例外点处。然而，相应的量子理论描述以及量子效应如何影响同步转变尚不清楚，需要从量子Liouvillian的角度研究这些动力学转变。

Method: 研究耦合激光器或凝聚体的量子理论，通过分析Liouvillian慢模式的谱和本征矩阵结构来表征不同稳态regime。追踪不同regime之间的谱流（spectral flow），识别控制同步转变的例外点级联结构。利用广义PT对称性来保护和解释这些例外点的出现。将量子Liouvillian的奇异性与经典相空间拓扑变化建立对应关系。

Result: 证明了经典极限下的不同稳态regime对应于Liouvillian慢模式的不同谱形式和本征矩阵结构。发现同步转变由受广义PT对称性保护的例外点级联控制。量子Liouvillian的奇异性决定了不同动力学regime的特征。即使在少粒子regime（量子效应占主导）下，同步转变的实验特征仍然保持良好定义。

Conclusion: 量子Liouvillian的奇异性，特别是其慢模式中的例外点级联，控制着耦合振荡器系统的不同动力学regime和同步转变。广义PT对称性保护这些例外点结构。研究结果将经典同步现象的量子理论描述与可观测的实验特征联系起来，并表明这些特征在量子效应显著的少粒子regime中依然适用，为理解和探测量子系统中的同步转变提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The coupling between self-sustained oscillators, such as lasers or polariton condensates, leads to different steady-states in different regimes. Weak coupling allows the oscillators to behave independently, with different frequencies and uncorrelated phases, while stronger coupling can produce synchronization. In classical or semiclassical theories transitions between these steady-states correspond to changes in the topology of the phase space attractors and occur at generalized exceptional points. We investigate the corresponding changes in a quantum theory of coupled lasers or condensates. We show how the different steady-state regimes of the classical limit give rise to distinct forms for the spectra and eigenmatrices of the slow modes of the Liouvillian. By following the spectral flow between the different regimes we show that the synchronization transition is controlled by cascades of exceptional points in these slow modes, protected by a generalized PT symmetry. Our results show how singularities of the quantum Liouvillian control the different dynamical regimes, and give rise to experimental signatures of the synchronization transition which remain well-defined in the few-particle regime dominated by quantum effects.

</details>


### [113] [Zero bias field selective transition adressing of the NV center via pulse shaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18887)
*Thomas Richard,Yves Bérubé-Lauzière*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于优化的脉冲整形控制框架,通过对驱动场的时域调制实现无需静态偏置磁场的氮空位(NV)中心系综的选择性操控,解决了简并NV系统中的光谱重叠问题,为高精度矢量磁力测量提供了新方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NV中心磁力计通常依赖外加偏置磁场来解除基态自旋能级简并,但高精度测量时偏置磁场会因热漂移、机械振动和磁滞效应引入系统误差。现有解决方案(光学极化标记、光学各向异性、核自旋耦合、圆偏振微波、特定脉冲序列等)存在局限性,需要开发无需静态偏置磁场的NV中心选择性操控新方法。

Method: 提出基于优化的脉冲整形控制框架,通过对驱动场进行最优时域调制,实现对NV中心系综的取向选择性(orientation-selective)和子空间选择性(subspace-selective)操控。该方法利用时间相关的脉冲设计来区分不同取向的NV中心及其自旋跃迁,无需外加静态偏置磁场打破简并态。

Result: 该优化脉冲整形方法成功实现了简并NV系统中的光谱重叠分辨,能够在没有静态偏置磁场的条件下对不同取向的NV中心进行选择性和鲁棒性操控,为解决传统方法中偏置磁场引入的系统误差提供了有效途径。

Conclusion: 基于优化的脉冲整形控制为金刚石NV中心矢量磁力测量提供了一种无需静态偏置磁场的新范式,通过时域调制驱动场实现取向和子空间的选择性操控,有效规避了偏置磁场带来的热漂移、机械振动和磁滞等系统误差,为高精度磁场测量开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers in diamond are a leading platform for vector magnetometry, offering intrinsic sensitivity to both the magnitude and direction of magnetic fields. NV magnetometers typically rely on an external bias field to lift the degeneracy of the ground-state spin sublevels, enabling spectral discrimination of different NV orientations and spin transitions. At high accuracy, however, such a bias field systematically introduces errors through thermal, mechanical, and hysteretic drifts, thus hindering the accurate measurement of the magnetic field of interest. Several strategies have been proposed to address this limitation, including optical polarization-based orientation labeling, optical anisotropy, strong coupling to nearby nuclear spins, circular microwave polarization, and tailored pulse sequences. In this work, we introduce an optimization-based pulse-shaping control framework that enables selective and robust manipulation of NV ensembles without relying on a static bias field. Our method achieves both orientation-selective and subspace-selective control through optimal temporal modulation of the driving fields, providing a route to resolving spectral overlaps in degenerate NV systems.

</details>


### [114] [Entanglement swapping across a five-node relay in a multiplexed quantum-classical network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18899)
*Andrew R. Cameron,Jordan M. Thomas,Alexandru Macridin,Si Xie,Raju Valivarthi,Soumya S. Ghosh,Yerko Muñoz Barros,Neil Sinclair,Panagiotis Spentzouris,Maria Spiropulu,Prem Kumar,Cristián Peña*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次演示了在同一光纤中同时实现纠缠交换和传统通信,通过五节点量子中继拓扑结构在40公里光纤上成功实现纠缠交换,同时在所有光纤中传输10 Gbps的经典数据,为量子网络与经典网络共存提供了重要技术突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络在扩展量子计算和分布式传感技术方面具有重要价值,但在实际基础设施部署时需要将量子比特与承载传统互联网通信的经典光信号复用到同一光纤中。纠缠交换是量子网络的核心技术,特别是通过量子中继器突破速率-损耗限制,但此前尚未实现纠缠交换与经典通信在同一光纤上的共存运行,这限制了量子技术的广泛部署。

Method: 采用密集波分复用(DWDM)技术将C波段时间片纠缠光子与C波段经典信号复用到同一光纤中。构建了由四段长距离光纤连接的五节点量子中继拓扑结构,每段光纤都承载经典数据信号。通过实验和理论方法表征了量子保真度与高功率经典光产生的拉曼噪声光子之间的权衡关系,系统分析了经典信号对量子态的影响。

Result: 成功在最长40公里的光纤(四段10公里光纤)上实现纠缠交换,同时在所有光纤中传输10 Gbps的经典数据。实验验证了量子保真度与拉曼噪声之间的定量关系,证明了通过适当的波长选择和功率控制可以在保持量子纠缠特性的同时实现高速经典通信。

Conclusion: 本研究首次实现了纠缠交换与传统通信在同一光纤基础设施上的共存运行,显著提升了量子-经典混合网络的复杂度演示水平。研究结果为量子技术的广泛部署提供了清晰的技术路线图,表明在现有光纤通信基础设施上部署先进量子网络技术具有可行性,为未来量子互联网与经典互联网的融合奠定了重要基础。

Abstract: Quantum networks are resources for scaling quantum computers and distributed sensing technologies while offering post-quantum security benefits. Teleporting non-classical resources like entanglement, via so called entanglement swapping, is essential for networks in particular overcoming rate-loss limits via quantum repeaters. Deploying these systems on real infrastructure will likely require multiplexing photonic qubits into fibers carrying 'classical' light encoding standard Internet communications and control plane signals for multi-node quantum protocols. Here, we report the first demonstration of entanglement swapping and conventional communications operating over the same fibers. Entanglement is swapped across a five-node quantum relay topology connected by four long-distance fibers, each populated with classical data signals. Time-bin entangled photons in the C-band are multiplexed alongside C-band classical signals using dense-wavelength division multiplexing, introducing noise photons generated by high-power classical light. We experimentally and theoretically characterize the trade-off between quantum fidelity and Raman noise photons. Entanglement swapping is demonstrated over a maximum fiber length of 40 km (four 10-km fibers) while simultaneously transmitting 10-Gbps classical data through all fibers. These results represent a significant advancement in the demonstrated complexity of coexisting quantum and classical networks and provide a roadmap for achieving the widespread deployment of advanced quantum technologies.

</details>


### [115] [Logarithmic-depth quantum simulation of boson sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18907)
*Changhun Oh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了玻色采样可以用对数深度的量子比特电路以逆多项式全变差误差进行模拟,从而将玻色采样归入浅层量子计算的范畴


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色采样作为量子优越性的候选方案,其计算复杂性一直是研究焦点。本文旨在探究玻色采样与浅层量子电路之间的关系,证明玻色采样的计算能力实际上可以被对数深度的量子比特电路实现,这对理解玻色采样的计算复杂性边界具有重要意义

Method: 核心方法包含四个关键步骤:1)扩展光学系统以获得更大的变换空间;2)将得到的变换分解为六个二次剪切变换;3)将每个模式分布到多个子模式上,实现模式的重新分配;4)通过这种重新分配引入固定的局部占据截断,然后应用局部基变换和并行相位门构建量子比特电路。该电路使用Clifford+T门、任意量子比特连接性和单次末态测量,其电路族具有对数空间一致性

Result: 成功构建了一个对数深度的量子比特电路,该电路使用多项式数量的量子比特,能够以逆多项式的全变差误差模拟任意m模干涉仪和n≤m个单光子输入的玻色采样问题。该电路具有对数深度、多项式量子比特数、使用标准Clifford+T门集的特性

Conclusion: 本研究将玻色采样明确地置于浅层量子计算的范畴内,表明玻色采样可以被对数深度的量子比特电路高效模拟。这一结果对理解玻色采样的计算能力边界具有重要理论意义,说明玻色采样的计算复杂性可能不如之前认为的那样难以触及

Abstract: We show that boson sampling with an arbitrary $m$-mode interferometer and $n\le m$ single-photon inputs can be simulated to inverse-polynomial total-variation error by a logarithmic-depth qubit circuit with polynomially many qubits. The circuit uses Clifford+$T$ gates, arbitrary qubit connectivity, and a single final measurement, and its family is logspace uniform. The key idea is to enlarge the optical system, decompose the resulting transformation into six quadratic shears, and distribute each mode over many submodes. This redistribution permits a fixed local occupation cutoff, after which local basis changes and parallel phase gates give the qubit circuit. Consequently, our result places boson sampling within shallow quantum computation.

</details>


### [116] [Hamiltonian engineering via pulses: beyond group averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18911)
*Ivan Beschastnyi,Lucah Patel,David Tinoco*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个几何框架，用于在有限维量子系统中使用理想控制脉冲进行哈密顿量工程设计，通过凸包和李代数结构刻画有效哈密顿量族，并应用于动力学解耦和量子比特网络控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的哈密顿量工程方法缺乏系统的几何描述框架，难以全面刻画理想控制脉冲下可实现的有效哈密顿量空间。需要一个统一的理论框架来描述超越群平均的可能性，并明确刻画可局部访问的有效哈密顿量族。

Method: 从具有无界控制振幅的双线性薛定谔方程出发，构造闭合脉冲群；利用控制系统扩展和Filippov松弛定理，得到由漂移项伴随轨道的凸包加脉冲群李代数构成的有效哈密顿量族；使用伴随表示的同型分解刻画轨道多胞体的仿射包；对连通阿贝尔脉冲群，通过限制根描述相关表示分解；针对具有相同成对耦合的量子比特网络进行应用分析。

Result: 建立了有效哈密顿量的几何刻画框架，证明群平均位于仿射包内部，得到漂移项不变部分周围局部可访问的有效哈密顿量族；恢复了从有限维环境的任意相互作用中实现动力学解耦的充要条件；对量子比特网络系统给出了可生成有效哈密顿量的定性表征和定量估计。

Conclusion: 该几何框架系统地描述了理想控制脉冲下有限维量子系统的哈密顿量工程能力，轨道多胞体的几何结构揭示了超越传统群平均方法的可能性。框架成功应用于动力学解耦问题，并为量子比特网络的有效哈密顿量设计提供了理论工具，为量子控制的几何理论提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We develop a geometric framework for Hamiltonian engineering in finite-dimensional quantum systems using ideal control pulses. Starting from a bilinear Schrödinger equation with unbounded control amplitudes, we construct the closed pulse group and use extensions of control systems and Filippov's relaxation theorem to obtain a family of effective Hamiltonians given by the convex hull of the drift's adjoint orbit plus the Lie algebra of the pulse group. The geometry of this orbitope describes possibilities beyond group averaging. Using the isotypic decomposition of the adjoint representation, we characterize its affine hull and show that the group average lies in its interior. This yields locally accessible families of effective Hamiltonians around the invariant part of the drift. We apply the framework to recover the necessary and sufficient condition for dynamical decoupling from arbitrary interactions with a finite-dimensional bath. For connected abelian pulse groups, we describe the relevant representation decomposition through restricted roots. Finally, for qubit networks with identical pairwise couplings, we give a qualitative characterization and quantitative estimation of effective Hamiltonians that can be generated using our framework.

</details>


### [117] [Exact logical error rates for magic state cultivation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18922)
*Kwok Ho Wan,Ainhoa Zapirain*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过泡利传播和二进制张量收缩方法，精确计算了距离d=3和d=5的魔态培养电路（来自Clifft和SOFT）的接受率和逻辑错误率，研究了实际的T门而非采样用的S门代理，并通过级数展开形式给出了逻辑错误率。结果表明d=3和d=5电路的实际容错距离分别为2和3，解释了距离退化效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的魔态培养电路（如Clifft和SOFT）主要通过采样方法使用S门代理来评估性能，缺乏对实际T门在所有故障阶数下的精确分析。需要准确计算这些电路的逻辑错误率和容错距离，以理解其在不同电路级噪声强度下的真实性能表现，特别是距离退化效应的根本原因。

Method: 采用泡利传播（Pauli propagation）和二进制张量收缩（binary tensor contraction）技术，对距离d=3和d=5的魔态培养电路进行精确计算。研究对象是实际的T门而非S门代理，计算涵盖多个电路级噪声强度p下的每个故障阶数。通过级数展开形式表示逻辑错误率，展开至(p/(1-p))^10阶，并分析电路的实际容错距离。

Result: 解析结果在d=3和d=5两种情况下都恢复了Clifft和SOFT的数值结果，误差在采样不确定性范围内。关键发现是d=3和d=5电路的实际容错距离分别为d_fault=2和d_fault=3，而非其名义距离。这一结果与文献[Quantum 10, 2134 (2026)]配套代码中观察到的距离退化效应一致。

Conclusion: 通过精确的解析计算验证了Clifft和SOFT魔态培养电路的性能评估，揭示了这些电路存在容错距离退化现象：d=3电路实际容错距离为2，d=5电路为3。这一发现为理解魔态培养电路的容错性能提供了理论依据，说明名义距离与实际容错距离之间存在系统性差距，对量子纠错码设计具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: We compute exactly the acceptance and logical error rates for the distance $d=3$ and $d=5$ magic state cultivation circuits from Clifft [arXiv:2604.27058] and SOFT [arXiv:2512.23037] using Pauli propagation and binary tensor contraction. Actual $T$ gates are studied, not the $S$-gate proxy used for sampling. The calculation includes every fault order at several circuit-level noise strengths ($p$). We provide a series expansion form to the logical error rates, through order $(p/(1-p))^{10}$. The analytical results recover the numerical values from Clifft and SOFT at both $d=3$ and $d=5$ to within their sampling uncertainty. Then, we show that the $d=3$ and $d=5$ circuits actually have fault distances of $d_{\text{fault}}=2$ and $d_{\text{fault}}=3$ respectively, explaining the similar distance degrading effects from the companion code of [Quantum 10, 2134 (2026)].

</details>


### [118] [Optimal Temporal Hiding in Correlated Quantum Reference-Frame Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18948)
*Maxim V. Churilov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究有限群值时间参考系定义的关联随机酉过程,将时间隐藏转化为q元编码问题。证明了在特定条件下完整轨迹律是精确的操作坐标,并建立了经典轨迹隐私与相干时间隐私的分离,量化了从简化过程层析中隐藏的鲁棒载荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子时间参考系中的关联随机酉过程不同于独立信道列表,需要研究其完整轨迹律作为操作坐标的条件。现有理论缺乏对时间隐藏问题的编码论刻画,以及经典轨迹隐私与量子相干时间隐私之间界限的精确描述。需要量化从部分时隙观测中隐藏信息载荷的能力,并建立与量子纠错码的联系。

Method: 1) 识别载波包含有限群G所有不可约表示时的特殊机制,利用单个辅助输入解析所有群元素;2) 证明策略半距离等于总变差距离d_TV(μ,ν),将最优因果后处理简化为G^n上的经典卷积;3) 将时间隐藏映射为q元编码问题,利用[n,k]_q码的均匀陪集律构造在少于d(C⊥)个时隙上不可区分但全局完全可区分的分布;4) 对嵌套码C⊂D建立载荷R、不可见深度t和扇区距离的界;5) 使用广义Reed-Solomon码达到存在范围内的界,并分析投影秩泄漏轮廓;6) 证明从t个时隙隐藏任意相干叠加等价于量子擦除纠错。

Result: 1) 对任意群G^n上的律μ,ν,策略半距离精确等于d_TV(μ,ν);2) 均匀陪集律在少于d(C⊥)个时隙完全不可区分;3) M个扇区在t个选定时隙泄漏最多η、全局解码错误ε时,满足(1-ε)log M ≤ (n-t)log q+η+h_2(ε);4) 嵌套码满足R+t+d_rel(D,C)-1≤n和R+t+2e+f≤n;5) 嵌套广义Reed-Solomon码在存在范围内达到界;6) 相干隐藏满足log_q K+2t≤n,给出精确投影秩泄漏轮廓。

Conclusion: 本文建立了有限群值时间参考系中时间隐藏的完整编码论框架,证明了在载波包含所有不可约表示时轨迹律成为精确操作坐标。通过将问题转化为q元编码,获得了载荷、不可见深度和距离参数的紧界,并用嵌套Reed-Solomon码实现。关键贡献是严格分离了经典轨迹隐私与量子相干时间隐私,揭示后者等价于量子擦除纠错,为量子过程层析的鲁棒隐私提供了信息论界限。

Abstract: A finite group-valued temporal reference frame defines a correlated random-unitary process rather than a list of independent channels. We identify a regime in which its complete trajectory law is an exact operational coordinate. If the carrier contains every irreducible representation of the finite group $G$, one ancilla-assisted input resolves all group elements, and for arbitrary laws $μ,ν$ on $G^n$ the strategy half-distance equals $d_{\mathrm{TV}}(μ,ν)$. Optimal output-side causal post-processing likewise reduces to classical convolution on $G^n$. This converts temporal hiding into a $q$-ary coding problem. Uniform coset laws of an $[n,k]_q$ code are identical on every set of fewer than $d(C^\perp)$ slots and perfectly distinguishable globally. For $M$ sectors with leakage at most $η$ from $t$ selected slots and global decoding error $ε$, we obtain $(1-ε)\log M \leq (n-t)\log q+η+h_2(ε)$. For nested codes $C\subset D$, the payload $R=\dim D-\dim C$, invisible depth $t=d_{\mathrm{rel}}(C^\perp,D^\perp)-1$, and sector distance $d_{\mathrm{rel}}(D,C)$ obey $R+t+d_{\mathrm{rel}}(D,C)-1\leq n$ and $R+t+2e+f\leq n$, where $e$ and $f$ are adversarial errors and known erasures. Nested generalized Reed-Solomon codes attain the bounds in their existence range. We also give the exact projection-rank leakage profile and show that hiding arbitrary coherent superpositions from $t$ slots is precisely quantum erasure correction, yielding $\log_q K+2t\leq n$. The results separate classical trajectory privacy from coherent temporal privacy and quantify the robust payload hidden from reduced process tomography.

</details>


### [119] [A Gauge Sign Rule for Quantum Rotor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18969)
*Swagata Acharya*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子旋转子网络中的符号问题，证明了电荷基哈密顿量无符号的充要条件是所有回路通量模2π为零，揭示了回路挫折通量作为唯一规范不变量控制符号障碍，并统一解释了Mott转变、紧致U(1)规范场论和受挫连续优化三类看似无关问题的符号来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统的符号问题(非正路径积分权重)使经典模拟变得困难，该问题出现在费米子反对称性、几何挫折、实时演化、拓扑角度和有限密度等看似无关的场景中。本文旨在针对紧致连续变量(量子旋转子网络模型)系统性研究符号问题的起源和规律，寻找控制符号障碍的统一规范不变量，并建立不同物理问题之间符号问题的内在联系。

Method: 1) 理论证明:针对量子旋转子哈密顿量建立符号规则(Marshall规则的旋转子类比),证明电荷基表示下无符号的充要条件;2) 精确对角化(Exact Diagonalization):数值验证符号代价作为规范不变量与通量的函数关系;3) 密度矩阵重整化群(DMRG):研究符号代价随系统尺寸的标度行为;4) 理论映射:通过slave-rotor映射等方法建立与Mott转变、紧致U(1)格点规范场论、受挫连续优化问题的联系;5) 物理实现:提出超导旋转子阵列作为该模型及其符号问题的原生实验平台。

Result: 1) 证明了单一规范不变量(相互作用图独立回路的挫折通量)控制符号障碍;2) 建立了符号规则:电荷基哈密顿量无符号当且仅当每个回路通量模2π为零;3) 数值结果显示:单回路符号代价在零通量处消失,在π处达到峰值,随回路周长指数衰减;多回路情况下符号代价是广延量,随系统尺寸线性增长;4) 统一解释:同一回路通量机制产生了Mott转变(通过slave-rotor映射)、紧致U(1)格点规范场论和受挫连续优化三个通常分开处理问题的符号;5) 超导旋转子阵列可原生实现该模型及其符号问题。

Conclusion: 本文为紧致连续变量量子系统建立了完整的符号问题理论框架,揭示回路挫折通量作为唯一规范不变控制量的核心地位。证明的符号规则为判断系统可模拟性提供了明确准则,数值结果揭示了符号代价从单回路的指数抑制到多回路广延性的演化规律。更重要的是,该工作建立了Mott转变、格点规范场论和受挫优化等不同物理问题符号来源的深层统一性,并指出超导量子器件可作为研究这一基本问题的实验平台,为理解和克服量子模拟中的符号障碍提供了新视角。

Abstract: The sign problem is the non-positive path-integral weight that makes a quantum system hard to simulate classically. It shows up in problems that look unrelated, from fermion antisymmetry and geometric frustration to real-time evolution, topological angles, and finite density. We study it here for compact continuous variables, modeled as networks of quantum rotors, $\hat H=\sum_i 4E_C(\hat n_i-n_{g,i})^2-\sum_{\langle ij\rangle} E_{ij}\cos(\hatφ_i-\hatφ_j-θ_{ij})$. For these, a single gauge-invariant quantity controls the obstruction, the frustration flux through the independent loops of the interaction graph. We prove a sign rule, the rotor analogue of Marshall's. The charge-basis Hamiltonian is sign-free if and only if every loop flux vanishes modulo $2π$. Exact diagonalization and density-matrix renormalization then show that the sign cost is a gauge-invariant function of the flux. For a single loop it vanishes at zero flux, peaks at $π$, and is exponentially small in the loop's perimeter. Summed over many loops it is extensive, growing with system size. The same loop flux generates the sign of three problems usually treated apart, namely the Mott transition (through the slave-rotor mapping), compact $U(1)$ lattice gauge theory, and frustrated continuous optimization. A superconducting rotor array realizes both the model and its sign natively.

</details>

*Yan Lu,Xiao-Feng Shi*

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于辅助比特暗态机制的超冷极性分子双量子比特纠缠门方案,可实现高保真度量子门操作,并具有扩展到多量子比特门的潜力,为可扩展量子计算提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管高保真纠缠门对基于电路的量子计算至关重要,但在可扩展平台上实现这类门仍然具有挑战性。光学捕获的超冷极性分子提供了一个潜在的可扩展平台,但需要新的机制来实现高保真度的量子门操作。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的辅助比特辅助暗态机制(ancilla-assisted dark-state mechanism)来实现双量子比特分子纠缠门。通过为辅助比特和数据量子比特选择合适的量子态,可以产生暗态,并且仅通过驱动辅助比特就能实现纠缠的生成。该机制利用暗态的特性避免了不需要的跃迁,从而提高门操作的保真度。

Result: 该方案能够实现高保真度的双量子比特纠缠门操作。研究还表明,通过相同的暗态机制,可以将双量子比特门扩展到多量子比特门,展示了方法的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 基于辅助比特暗态机制的超冷极性分子纠缠门方案为实现高保真、可扩展的量子计算提供了一条有前景的技术路线。该方案不仅适用于双量子比特门,还可扩展至多量子比特门,为构建基于极性分子的量子计算平台奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: High-fidelity entangling gates are useful for circuit-based quantum computing, yet their realization in a scalable platform is challenging. Optically trapped ultracold polar molecules offer a potentially scalable platform. Here, we introduce a two-qubit molecular entangling gate via a novel ancilla-assisted dark-state mechanism, where high fidelity is achievable. In particular, we find that by using appropriate qubit states for the ancilla and the data qubits, dark states can appear, and entanglement can emerge by driving the ancilla only. It is also feasible to extend the two-qubit gate to a multi-qubit gate via the same dark-state mechanism.

</details>


### [121] [Code-Visible Liouvillian Modes and Coherent Logical Errors in Quantum Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18978)
*Marina Gordeychuk,Oleg Kiselev*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从线性系统理论的角度研究量子纠错中相干哈密顿漂移和系统误差的传播特性，提出了最小码可见空间的概念，并通过可观测性和最小实现理论分析了逻辑信道的动力学结构，特别关注相干噪声的谱特征及其对解码器选择的依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相干哈密顿漂移和系统误差在量子纠错中的传播方式与随机泡利噪声不同，它们会产生在完全泡利旋转下丢失的逻辑信道相位分量。现有研究缺乏系统化的理论框架来描述这些相干误差如何通过编码-恢复-解码方案传播，以及如何依赖于解码器的选择。因此需要从可观测性和最小实现的角度建立方法论工具来分析这类问题。

Method: 1) 将线性系统理论中的可观测性和最小实现概念应用于量子纠错的逻辑信道分析；2) 定义最小码可见空间为可解码逻辑可观测量关于Lindblad算子伴随的Krylov闭包；3) 通过可观测分解得到逻辑信道的最小实现；4) 将Knill-Laflamme条件作为短时一致性检验；5) 对稳定子码上的泡利噪声简化为综合征-逻辑类上的链式结构；6) 分析恢复映射作为可见模式的谱滤波器的作用；7) 用逻辑信道主导可见本征值估计量子存储时间。

Result: 建立了基于可观测性理论的量子纠错逻辑信道分析框架，明确了最小码可见空间的数学结构及其可观测分解形式。证明了在该框架下：Knill-Laflamme条件对应短时行为的一致性；稳定子码上泡利噪声可简化为综合征-逻辑类的马尔可夫链；恢复映射对可见模式具有谱滤波效应。获得了基于主导可见本征值的量子存储时间估计方法，揭示了相干噪声的谱特征及其对解码器的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 通过引入线性系统理论的可观测性和最小实现方法，本文为量子纠错中相干误差的传播分析提供了系统化的理论框架。最小码可见空间的概念有效刻画了逻辑信道的动力学结构，揭示了恢复映射作为谱滤波器的本质作用。该方法不仅统一了Knill-Laflamme条件和稳定子码噪声模型的理解，还为量子存储时间估计和相干噪声谱特征分析提供了实用工具，强调了解码器选择对相干误差可见性的关键影响。

Abstract: Coherent Hamiltonian drifts and systematic errors can pass through quantum error correction differently than stochastic Pauli noise: they generate phase components of the logical channel that are lost under full Pauli twirling. We consider the Lindbladian dynamics of \(n\) physical qubits propagated through an effective logical channel of a fixed encoding, recovery, and decoding scheme. The work is methodological in character: standard ideas of observability and minimal realization from linear systems theory are applied to the channel \[
  ω
  \mapsto
  Φ_t(ω)
  =
  \cD e^{t\cL}\cE(ω), \] where \(\cE\) encodes the logical state and \(\cD\) is the chosen recovery map with final decoding. The minimal code-visible space \(\cK_{\rm code}\) is defined as the Krylov closure of the decodable logical observables with respect to \(\cL^\dagger\); its observable factorization yields a minimal realization of the logical channel. In this form, the Knill--Laflamme conditions serve as a short-time consistency check, Pauli noise on stabilizer codes reduces to a chain on syndrome-logical classes, and the choice of recovery map acts as a spectral filter on the visible modes. For quantum memory, the same language yields an estimate of the storage time in terms of the dominant visible eigenvalues of the logical channel of the correction cycle. Particular attention is paid to the spectral signatures of coherent noise and to how these signatures depend on the chosen decoder.

</details>


### [122] [Shor's Conjecture Is True: Projective Measurements Suffice for Binary Accessible Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19003)
*Sunghyeon Jo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造性地证明了Shor猜想：冯·诺依曼测量在任意有限维度下都能达到二元量子系综的可达信息量。通过构造算子T_M及其谱投影测量Π_M，证明了投影测量优于一般POVM测量，并给出了可达信息的精确凹变分公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shor猜想提出冯·诺依曼投影测量（PVM）能够达到二元量子系综的可达信息量，但缺乏构造性证明。需要在任意有限维度下严格证明投影测量相比一般正算子值测量（POVM）在提取量子信息方面的优越性，并建立可达信息的精确数学表达式。

Method: 对于任意有限结果的POVM测量M，从后验标签概率构造算子T_M，并从中导出其谱投影值测量（PVM）Π_M。通过两次应用Jensen算子不等式，证明Π_M的互信息不小于M的互信息（I_{Π_M}(X:Y)≥I_M(X:Y)），并证明任何秩一细化都保持该不等式。这种构造性方法明确了替换映射M→T_M→Π_M的过程，隔离了二元情况的论证。

Result: 成功证明了在任意有限维度下，投影值测量Π_M的互信息始终不低于原POVM测量M的互信息。建立了可达信息的精确凹变分公式。证明过程明确展示了从一般POVM到最优投影测量的构造性转换，且该结果对所有秩一细化都保持成立。

Conclusion: 本文构造性地证明了Shor猜想在任意有限维度下成立，确认了冯·诺依曼投影测量在二元量子系综中提取可达信息的最优性。该结果是Fang、Fawzi和Fawzi关于测量f-散度一般定理的特殊情况，但本文的证明方法明确隔离了二元论证，使得从POVM到最优PVM的替换过程变得显式和可构造。

Abstract: Shor conjectured that a von Neumann measurement attains the accessible information of every binary quantum ensemble. We prove the conjecture constructively in arbitrary finite dimension. For every finite-outcome positive operator-valued measure (POVM) $M$, we form an operator $T_M$ from the posterior label probabilities and show that its spectral projection-valued measure (PVM) $Π_M$ satisfies $I_{Π_M}(X{:}Y)\ge I_M(X{:}Y)$; every rank-one refinement retains the inequality. Two applications of Jensen's operator inequality prove the comparison and yield an exact concave variational formula for the accessible information. The result is a special case of the general theorem of Fang, Fawzi, and Fawzi on measured $f$-divergences; the proof below isolates the binary argument and makes the replacement $M\mapsto T_M\mapstoΠ_M$ explicit.

</details>


### [123] [Quantum-Classical Effective Fragment Potential Embedding for Condensed-Phase Quantum Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19018)
*Federico Zahariev,Vassiliki-Alexandra Glezakou,Mark S. Gordon*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了量子-经典有效片段势(Q-EFP)方法,通过将化学活性区域用量子算法处理而环境用经典有效片段势表示,成功将溶剂化体系的量子比特需求降低了10-35倍,为在真实环境中进行量子化学模拟提供了一种高效的混合量子-经典策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子化学模拟在处理真实环境(溶剂、离子等)时面临巨大挑战,因为显式处理环境分子会导致轨道数量激增,需要大量量子比特资源。现有量子计算资源有限,难以模拟包含完整溶剂化环境的化学体系。因此需要开发一种能够分离环境规模与量子寄存器规模的方法,在保持计算精度的同时大幅降低量子比特需求。

Method: Q-EFP方法采用量子-经典混合策略:1)将体系划分为化学活性区和环境区,活性区用量子算法(如UCCSD)处理;2)环境区用GAMESS中的经典有效片段势(EFP)表示;3)库仑势和极化势被纳入活性区的单电子哈密顿量;4)EFP-EFP相互作用和短程环境贡献通过经典方法组装;5)通过Q-GAMESS工作流实现嵌入式哈密顿量的构建。该方法使用statevector UCCSD/STO-3G进行基准测试。

Result: 在三个测试体系中验证了方法的有效性:1)水/甲醇混合溶剂中的LiH,与经典CCSD/EFP计算偏差仅0.03 kcal/mol,量子比特从约84个降至4个;2)五水分子环境中的H2O,偏差0.38 kcal/mol;3)铵/硝酸盐环境中的BeH2,偏差0.01 kcal/mol,量子比特从约246个降至8个。总体实现了约10-35倍的量子寄存器规模缩减,同时保持了与经典高精度方法相当的计算精度。

Conclusion: Q-EFP概念验证结果成功验证了嵌入式哈密顿量和Q-GAMESS工作流的可行性,实现了环境规模与量子寄存器规模的有效分离。该方法与实空间Q-EFMO片段化和虚轨道Q-FVO约化方法互补,为处理更大规模的溶剂化体系提供了分层策略。Q-EFP为在近期量子计算设备上模拟真实环境中的化学反应开辟了实用途径,展示了量子-经典混合方法在量子化学模拟中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Quantum simulation of chemistry in realistic environments is constrained by the orbital cost of explicit solvent, ions, and other surroundings. We present the quantum-classical effective fragment potential (Q-EFP) method, in which a chemically active region is treated with a quantum algorithm and the environment is represented by the effective fragment potential (EFP) in GAMESS. Coulomb and polarization potentials enter the active-region one-electron Hamiltonian, while EFP-EFP and short-range environment contributions are assembled classically. Statevector UCCSD/STO-3G benchmarks for LiH in a mixed water/methanol environment, H2O in a five-water environment, and BeH2 in an ammonium/nitrate environment differ from matched classical CCSD/EFP calculations by 0.03, 0.38, and 0.01 kcal/mol, respectively. The active calculations require 4 to 8 qubits, compared with estimated full-system counts of approximately 84 to 246 qubits, corresponding to register reductions of about 10-fold to 35-fold. These proof-of-concept results validate the embedded Hamiltonian and Q-GAMESS workflow while separating environmental size from quantum-register size. Q-EFP is complementary to real-space Q-EFMO fragmentation and virtual-orbital Q-FVO reduction, enabling a layered route to larger solvated systems.

</details>


### [124] [Conditioning on the Future: A Filtration-Theoretic Formalization of Wheeler's Participatory Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19025)
*Chon-Fai Kam,Kai-Wen Wong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过经典条件理论(Doob h-变换、马尔可夫桥)和量子双态矢量形式化框架,论证了Wheeler延迟选择实验中的时间对称性是选择效应而非逆向因果,将"未来的拉力"解释为桥漂移现象,澄清了量子力学中参与性语言的合理范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子理论中的时间对称性(特别是Wheeler延迟选择实验和量子测量的时间对称形式)长期引发关于是否蕴含逆向因果性的争论。该争论混淆了两个不同的结构,需要一个清晰的框架来区分时间对称的条件演算与因果结构,明确延迟选择究竟是选择效应还是真正的逆向因果作用。

Method: 建立经典条件理论与量子形式化之间的对应字典:经典侧使用Doob h-变换、马尔可夫桥和滤波扩大理论;量子侧使用前向态/后向效应(双态矢量)形式化处理预选择和后选择系统。将Wheeler的参与性宇宙、延迟选择和"It from Bit"提炼为三个命题,并在两个形式化体系中建立精确映射。核心论证基于结构同构结果:量子侧通过POVM完备性和迹保持性质,经典侧通过条件期望的塔性质,证明对最终测量边缘化不改变早期边缘分布。

Result: 证明了延迟选择是选择效应而非逆向因果:未来测量选择将过去系综分类为子系综而不扰动任何早期边缘分布。表观的"未来拉力"实际上是桥漂移。条件演算具有时间对称性,但其因果结构不具有时间对称性。明确指出该框架的局限:既不推导Born规则也不解决测量问题,并界定了量子力学中参与性语言的合理适用范围。

Conclusion: 通过经典-量子对应字典澄清了量子时间对称性与因果性的关系:时间对称的条件演算不等同于逆向因果。Wheeler延迟选择实验的本质是统计选择效应,未来测量通过条件化机制重组过去系综但不改变边缘概率,这一现象在经典和量子框架中结构同构。该工作为量子测量的参与性解释提供了精确的数学边界,区分了合法的统计解释与不必要的因果倒转假设。

Abstract: Wheeler's delayed-choice experiments and the time-symmetric formalisms of quantum measurement have long fueled a debate over whether time symmetry in quantum theory entails retrocausality. We argue that the debate conflates two distinct structures, and that the framework for separating them is the classical theory of conditioning: Doob $h$-transforms, Markov bridges, and enlargement of filtrations on the probabilistic side, and the forward-state/backward-effect (two-state-vector) formalism of pre- and post-selected systems on the quantum side. We sharpen Wheeler's participatory universe, delayed choice, and ``It from Bit'' into three theses and build a correspondence dictionary that maps each to a precise statement in one or both formalisms. The dictionary is anchored by two structurally isomorphic results: an operational proposition that a future measurement choice sorts the past ensemble into subensembles without disturbing any earlier marginal, and its classical counterpart, the disintegration of an unconditioned law into future-conditioned bridges -- both a single fact: marginalizing over the final measurement leaves earlier marginals fixed, by completeness of the POVM together with trace preservation on the quantum side and by the tower property of conditional expectation on the classical. The conditional calculus is time-symmetric; its causal structure is not. Delayed choice is thereby a selection effect rather than retrocausation, and the apparent ``pull from the future'' is a bridge drift. We state explicitly what the framework does not do -- it neither derives the Born rule nor solves the measurement problem -- and delineate the legitimate scope of participatory language in quantum mechanics.

</details>


### [125] [Environment-aware transverse transport geometry for non-Gaussian oscillator states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19026)
*Arnaud Coatanhay,Angelique Dremeau*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种截断Fock空间上的方向依赖局部传输代价度量，通过商掉哈密顿切向分量构造横向半范数，并利用加权算符框架来区分可逆相位旋转与不可逆环境退相干过程，为非高斯振子态提供环境敏感的局部诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非高斯振子态对微弱扰动高度敏感，但可逆的相位旋转和不可逆的环境变化不应被等同对待。现有度量方法缺乏对这两类演化的有效区分，且难以在有限维截断Fock空间中准确刻画多光子过程和退相干等物理过程的代价差异。因此需要构造一种能够识别物理演化方向、区分哈密顿演化与耗散动力学、并具有明确截断和正则化约定的局部度量工具。

Method: 在截断的Fock空间上引入有限维、方向依赖的局部传输代价，通过商掉哈密顿切向方向得到横向半范数（而非全局距离）。采用加权算符框架来指定物理流的表示代价：基础框架{a_M, a_M†}已张成全部迹为零的扇区，但通过引入多光子算符和对角方向，可以更高效地表示选定的Lindblad切向量。利用算符-模协变性处理热权重，构建具有明确截断、正则化和归一化约定的环境感知局部诊断量。

Result: 对指南针类态的数值测试表明：(1)相位旋转被成功移除；(2)仅在包含对角方向后退相干过程才变得代价低廉；(3)双光子和三光子过程能被多光子框架自然捕获。热权重满足算符-模协变性，但不构成完整的KMS几何结构。所提出的横向半范数能够有效区分不同物理过程的代价特征。

Conclusion: 本文成功构造了一种环境感知的局部诊断工具，能够在截断Fock空间中区分可逆哈密顿演化与不可逆环境退相干。该方法通过方向依赖的传输代价和加权算符框架，为非高斯量子态的演化分析提供了具有明确物理意义和数学规范的半范数度量，而非传统的全局距离度量。该工具在量子光学和开放量子系统研究中具有实用价值。

Abstract: Non-Gaussian oscillator states are highly sensitive to weak perturbations, but reversible phase rotations and irreversible environmental changes should not be weighted equally. We introduce a finite-dimensional, direction-dependent local transport cost on a truncated Fock space and quotient it by Hamiltonian tangent directions. The resulting transverse quantity is a seminorm, not a global distance.
  A weighted operator frame specifies the representation cost of physical fluxes. Although the frame ({a_M,a_M^\dagger}) already spans the full trace-zero sector, multiphoton and diagonal directions can represent selected Lindblad tangents much more efficiently. Numerical tests on compass-like states show that phase rotations are removed, dephasing becomes inexpensive only after diagonal directions are included, and two- and three-photon processes are naturally captured by multiphoton frames. Thermal weights satisfy an operator-modular covariance but do not define a complete KMS geometry. The construction is therefore an environment-aware local diagnostic with explicit cutoff, regularization, and normalization conventions.

</details>


### [126] [Time of arrival in the semiclassical regime for Gaussian wave packets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19027)
*Mathieu Beau,Maximilien Barbier,Vincent Bucourt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了一维二次哈密顿量(含时频率的受迫谐振子)在半经典regime下高斯波包的到达时间(TOA)分布,导出了平均到达时间和标准差的紧凑闭式表达式,并将理论应用于自由落体和含时开口陷阱模型并通过数值积分验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自由落体的到达时间预测无法应用于实际系统中不可忽略束缚势的情况,需要将半经典到达时间分布理论扩展到更一般的含时哈密顿系统,特别是受迫谐振子这类具有时变频率的实际物理场景。

Method: 将高斯概率密度展开为Dirac delta函数导数的级数形式,利用分布函数与经典轨迹复合的标准规则,结合Ermakov宽度的时间导数、经典速度和加速度等物理量,推导半经典近似下的到达时间统计特性,得到平均到达时间的量子修正(σ²阶)和标准差的简化形式Δ𝒯_x≃σ(t_x)/v_x。

Result: 获得了领先阶半经典平均到达时间及其标准差的紧凑闭式表达式;标准差简化为仅依赖经典运动方程基本解的形式;导出了时间-位置不确定性关系;在自由落体和两种重力场中含时开口陷阱模型上应用该框架,所有表达式均通过TOA分布的精确数值积分得到验证。

Conclusion: 成功将半经典到达时间分布理论从自由落体扩展到一般一维二次哈密顿量系统,揭示了量子修正的物理起源(经典速度、加速度和Ermakov宽度时间导数),建立了仅依赖经典运动方程解的时间-位置不确定性关系,为处理实际束缚势不可忽略的现实物理系统提供了有效分析工具。

Abstract: We study the time-of-arrival (TOA) distribution of a Gaussian wave packet in the semiclassical regime for a general one-dimensional quadratic Hamiltonian, namely a forced harmonic oscillator with time-dependent frequency. Expanding the Gaussian probability density as a series in derivatives of the Dirac delta and using the standard rules for the composition of distributions with the classical trajectory, we obtain compact closed-form expressions for the leading semiclassical mean arrival time and its standard deviation. The mean acquires a quantum shift of order $σ^2$ set by the classical velocity and acceleration and by the time derivative of the Ermakov width, while the spread reduces to the simple form $Δ\mathcal{T}_x\simeqσ(t_x)/v_x$. The latter implies a time--position uncertainty relation that depends only on the fundamental solutions of the classical equation of motion. We apply the framework to free fall and to two models of a time-dependent opening trap under gravity, and we validate every expression against exact numerical integration of the TOA distribution. These results extend earlier free-fall predictions to realistic settings in which the trapping potential cannot be neglected.

</details>


### [127] [Quantum-Classical Fragmentation with the Effective Fragment Molecular Orbital Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19045)
*Federico Zahariev,Vassiliki-Alexandra Glezakou,Mark S. Gordon*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了量子有效片段分子轨道(Q-EFMO)方法,这是一种混合框架,通过变分量子本征求解器评估选定分子片段的关联能,并通过尺寸一致的EFMO能量表达式组装它们。该方法将所需的最大量子寄存器由整个体系大小降低到最大片段或片段对的大小,在LiH基准测试中,使用12个量子比特即可达到接近完整CCSD参考的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子计算资源受限,直接对大型分子体系进行全量子化学计算需要大量量子比特。为了降低量子资源需求,同时保持计算精度,需要开发一种能够将大体系分解为较小片段,并用有限量子比特高效处理的混合量子-经典方法,使量子化学计算在近期量子设备上具有实用性。

Method: Q-EFMO方法结合了经典Hartree-Fock EFMO计算和量子VQE/UCCSD计算。首先,通过Hartree-Fock EFMO计算提供单体参考态、多体极化效应和远距离片段对的EFP相互作用;然后,对单体和选定的近场二聚体独立执行VQE/UCCSD计算以获得关联能增量;最后通过尺寸一致的EFMO能量表达式组装总能量。采用了两种轨道约化方案来进一步减少量子比特需求,使最大量子寄存器由最大活性片段或片段对决定,而非整个体系大小。

Result: 在三层LiH的STO-3G基组基准测试中,对2.5至3.5埃的完整分离距离扫描显示,两种轨道约化方案都单调接近完整CCSD参考结果。较激进的约化方案在分离距离≥2.6埃时误差保持在1 kcal/mol以内,在3.5埃处误差仅为0.10 kcal/mol。该方案对单体使用6个量子比特,最大二聚体使用12个量子比特,而冻核二聚体基线需要14个量子比特。实现特定的成本代理从1000降低到40,减少了25倍。

Conclusion: Q-EFMO方法成功建立了将片段化策略与量子计算结合的理论框架、软件路径和资源标度关系。无噪声结果表明该方法能够显著减少量子资源需求(量子比特数和计算成本均大幅降低),同时保持接近完整CCSD的精度。这为在近期量子设备上处理较大分子体系提供了可行途径。但作者指出,更广泛的化学验证、更大基组测试以及实际硬件测试仍然是必要的后续工作。

Abstract: We present the quantum effective fragment molecular orbital (Q-EFMO) method, a hybrid framework that evaluates selected molecular-fragment correlation energies with the variational quantum eigensolver and assembles them through the size-consistent EFMO energy expression. A Hartree-Fock EFMO calculation supplies monomer references, many-body polarization, and distant-pair EFP interactions; independent VQE/UCCSD calculations provide correlation increments for monomers and selected near-field dimers. Consequently, the maximum quantum register is determined by the largest active fragment or fragment pair rather than by total system size. For a three-layer LiH benchmark in STO-3G, a full 2.5 to 3.5 Angstrom separation scan shows that two orbital-reduction schemes approach the full CCSD reference monotonically. The more aggressive scheme remains below 1 kcal/mol for separations of 2.6 Angstrom and greater and reaches 0.10 kcal/mol at 3.5 Angstrom, using 6 qubits for monomers and 12 for the largest dimers, compared with 14 qubits for the frozen-core dimer baseline. An implementation-specific cost proxy decreases from 1000 to 40, a factor of 25. These noise-free results establish the equations, software path, and resource scaling; broader chemical validation, larger bases, and hardware tests remain necessary.

</details>


### [128] [Coherence-generating power deviation: Fluctuations and input selectivity beyond average coherence generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19046)
*Kyoungho Cho,Jeongho Bang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入相干生成功率偏差(CGPD)作为量子过程相干生成能力的波动度量,将相干生成从平均值扩展为景观分析,揭示了拓扑和局域化等结构对相干生成选择性的影响,这些特征在平均相干生成功率中是不可见的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的相干生成功率(CGP)仅描述量子过程从非相干输入产生相干的平均能力,但平均值会掩盖相干来源的差异性。需要引入新的度量来区分稳健的全局相干生成与对特定输入群体不平衡的选择性转换,从而揭示量子过程在不同输入状态下的相干生成行为差异。

Method: 引入相干生成功率偏差(CGPD),即在非相干态均匀单纯形上生成的Hilbert-Schmidt相干的标准差。通过将幺正相干生成表示为布居空间上的二次响应函数,在任意有限维度下获得精确的一阶和二阶矩。将不可避免的各向同性波动与额外的各向异性分离,证明每个非平凡幺正生成器必定存在波动,并将该方法自然扩展到幺正量子信道。

Result: 理论证明了任何非平凡幺正算符都必然产生相干生成波动,成功区分了平均强度和输入选择性。通过量子行走和准周期输运的配对实例表明,拓扑结构和局域化可以在保持CGP不变的情况下显著改变CGPD,揭示了仅通过平均值无法观察到的量子系统结构特征。这种区分对基准测试、可靠性保证、输入系综敏感性和四副本测量协议具有直接影响。

Conclusion: CGPD将相干生成从单一平均指标扩展为完整的景观描述,成功区分了稳健的全局相干生成能力与对特定输入的选择性。小CGPD值表示跨输入布居的稳健生成,大值则揭示选择性转换特性。这一框架不仅适用于幺正演化,还可扩展到幺正量子信道,为量子相干资源理论提供了更精细的表征工具,揭示了拓扑和局域化等物理机制在相干生成中的深层作用。

Abstract: Coherence-generating power (CGP) tells how much coherence a quantum process produces on average from incoherent inputs, but an average can hide where that coherence comes from. We introduce the coherence-generating power deviation (CGPD), the standard deviation of the generated Hilbert--Schmidt coherence over the uniform simplex of incoherent states. CGPD turns coherence generation into a landscape: a small value signals robust production across input populations, whereas a large value reveals selective conversion of particular population imbalances. By representing unitary coherence generation as a quadratic response on population space, we obtain exact first and second moments in arbitrary finite dimension. We prove that every nontrivial unitary generator must fluctuate, and we separate this unavoidable isotropic fluctuation from excess anisotropy. The resulting distinction between average strength and input selectivity has direct consequences for benchmarking, reliability guarantees, input-ensemble susceptibility, and four-copy measurement protocols; it also extends naturally to unital quantum channels. Matched quantum-walk and quasiperiodic-transport examples show that topology and localization can leave CGP unchanged while substantially changing CGPD, revealing structure invisible to the mean alone.

</details>


### [129] [Securing quantum error correction against misleading advice from AI agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19090)
*A. Barış Özgüler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子纠错系统中AI顾问可能被攻击者利用来推送有害更新的安全问题，提出了基于校准测量的认证更新方案，通过校准态的逻辑测量来解决被动校正子记录的模糊性，并建立了在不确定性和漂移条件下拒绝有害更新同时保留有益更新的条件保证机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子纠错系统中，被动校正子记录存在固有的模糊性（例如相反的相干X旋转会产生相同的校正子历史分布），这使得恢复操作的选择面临困难。当AI顾问可能受到攻击者影响时，如何在存在不确定性和系统漂移的情况下，安全地接受有益的纠错更新而拒绝有害更新，成为一个关键的安全问题。需要一种机制来提供缺失的符号信息，并在不假设顾问建议正确的前提下认证更新的安全性。

Method: 提出了基于校准测量的认证更新框架：(1)在已知编码的校准态上执行终端逻辑测量，以提供被动校正子记录中缺失的符号信息；(2)引入独立的评估器，仅当校准不确定性和合理的漂移界限能够认证相对于当前恢复方案的改进时才接受更新；(3)建立校准置信度检查机制，在模拟的建议攻击场景中测试其有效性；(4)推导出校准数据年龄的充分限制条件，确保在部署过程中的持续改进；(5)在环面码和表面码上进行仿真实验，包括随机电路故障和噪声变化场景。

Result: 仿真实验表明：(1)校准置信度检查成功拒绝了有害提案，同时在诚实建议下保留了有益更新；(2)在匹配的仿真中，经过验证的信道特定界限在考虑评估时间后比通用界限保留了更多有益更新，同时在规定的漂移假设下阻止了测试的有害激活；(3)确定性控制器用相同的观测至少实现了同样多的有益更新；(4)违反漂移假设会导致环面码实验中出现有害接受；(5)量化了通过保守接受策略所放弃的恢复改进程度。

Conclusion: 本研究明确了量子纠错恢复选择所需的信息要素，建立了针对有害更新的条件保证机制。通过校准测量和独立评估器的组合，可以在AI顾问可能受到攻击的情况下，实现安全的量子纠错更新。结果证明了在不确定性和系统漂移存在的情况下，通过适当的校准置信度检查和漂移界限，能够在拒绝有害更新的同时保留有益更新。同时也量化了保守接受策略的代价，为量子计算系统的安全运维提供了理论基础和实践指导。

Abstract: Can an attacker turn influence over an artificial intelligence (AI) adviser into a harmful quantum error-correction update? We identify an ambiguity in passive syndrome records that obstructs recovery selection, then show how additional calibration measurements support certified recovery updates under uncertainty and drift. In an odd-distance square toric code with error-free preparation, syndrome measurements, and recovery operations, opposite coherent $X$ rotations produce identical passive syndrome-history distributions. Yet a fixed phase correction can help at one sign and harm at the other. A terminal logical measurement on known encoded calibration states supplies the missing sign information. A separate evaluator accepts an update only when calibration uncertainty and a justified drift bound certify improvement over the current recovery, without assuming that the adviser recommends correctly. In simulated advice attacks, calibration-confidence checks reject harmful proposals while retaining beneficial updates under honest advice. We derive sufficient limits on calibration age that require improvement through deployment. In matched simulations, a validated channel-specific bound retains more beneficial updates than the general bound after accounting for evaluation time, while preventing the tested harmful activations under the stated drift assumption. A separate surface-code experiment includes stochastic circuit faults and noise changing during acquisition. Deterministic controllers achieve at least as many beneficial updates with the same observations. Violating the drift assumption permits harmful acceptance in the toric experiment. The results identify information required for recovery selection, establish conditional guarantees against harmful updates, and quantify the recovery improvements forgone through conservative acceptance.

</details>


### [130] [Continuous variable distributed quantum sensing in integrated photonics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19092)
*Bethany Puzio,Oliver M. Green,Joel F. Tasker,Jonathan Frazer,Tamzin Ellis,Benjamin D. J. Sayers,Rachel N. Clark,Alex S. Clark,Giacomo Ferranti,Jonathan C. F. Matthews*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在集成光子芯片上实现了四模纠缠态的分布式量子传感,利用压缩光态实现了超越散粒噪声极限0.199(16) dB的量子增强精度,证明了集成光子平台在可扩展量子传感中的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分布式量子传感实验受限于体光学架构,缺乏可扩展性。尽管压缩光态是实验演示纠缠增强传感的理想资源,但需要一个紧凑、可扩展的平台来实现实际应用。集成光子学可以提供这样的平台,使量子传感器小型化和规模化成为可能。

Method: 在集成光子芯片上生成四模纠缠态,利用四个集成的零差探测器阵列在芯片上进行纠缠验证和相位传感。通过测量四个非定域相移的线性函数组合,实现纠缠增强的分布式量子传感。整个系统包括纠缠态制备、参数编码和测量都在集成光子电路中完成。

Result: 实现了超越散粒噪声极限0.199(16) dB的纠缠增强精度,而可分离态仅获得0.041(18) dB的精度提升。成功在芯片上生成、验证四模纠缠态,并完成了四个相移线性函数的量子增强传感,证明了集成光子平台的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究首次在集成光子芯片上实现了多模纠缠增强的分布式量子传感,突破了传统体光学系统的可扩展性限制。集成光子平台为实现紧凑、可扩展的量子传感网络提供了可行路径,为未来量子网络在精密测量领域的实际应用奠定了技术基础。

Abstract: Distributed quantum sensing is an emerging application of quantum networking, where entangled probe states are employed to sense combinations of delocalized parameters with enhanced precision relative to using separable states. Squeezed states of light are a prime resource for experimental demonstrations of entanglement-enhanced sensing, because they can be generated and entangled deterministically. Existing distributed quantum sensing experiments have been fundamentally limited in scalability due to their bulk-optic architectures. Meanwhile, integrated photonics provides a scalable and compact platform for quantum sensors. Here we demonstrate entanglement-enhanced sensing of linear functions of four phase shifts in an integrated photonic circuit. We find an entanglement-enhanced precision of 0.199(16) dB below the shot noise limit compared to 0.041(18) dB for separable states. A four-mode entangled state is generated on-chip with entanglement verification and phase sensing also performed on-chip with an array of four integrated homodyne detectors.

</details>


### [131] [Comparing magic state cultivation methods using matrix product states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19116)
*Tom Hartweg,Asier Piñeiro Orioli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用矩阵乘积态(MPS)方法精确计算了两种fold-transversal魔态培养方案的性能,并提出了一种新的部分容错方案,在d=5时实现了~1.3倍更低的预期时空成本,同时达到10^-9逻辑错误率。研究还开发了基于Pauli传播的预筛选方法,将所需精确模拟次数降低了三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 魔态培养可以以较低的预期时空成本制备高保真度魔态,但由于非Clifford电路难以模拟,一些方案的确切性能尚未确定。需要精确评估现有方案的实际表现,并探索更优化的魔态培养方法以降低量子计算的资源开销。

Method: 采用基于矩阵乘积态(MPS)和Clifford增强MPS(CAMPS)的方法精确模拟两类fold-transversal培养方案:(i)基于常规表面码S门的Sahay等人方案;(ii)基于部分容错fold-transversal S门的新提出方案。开发了基于简单Pauli传播的预筛选方法加速模拟,并使用子集采样等与模拟器无关的采样方法。将MPS/CAMPS性能与近期的near-Clifford模拟器Clifft进行了对比。

Result: 对于d=5的Sahay方案,|T⟩输出达到与传统用作廉价全Clifford代理的|S⟩输出相似的逻辑错误率,这与Gidney等人的d=5色码方案报告的约10倍差异形成对比。新提出的d=5方案在达到10^-9逻辑错误率的同时,预期时空成本降低约1.3倍。MPS和CAMPS在最困难的d=5常规表面码方案上的性能与Clifft相当甚至更优。基于Pauli传播的预筛选方法将所需精确模拟次数降低最多三个数量级。

Conclusion: 本研究通过MPS方法精确量化了魔态培养方案的性能,证明了表面码方案相比色码方案具有更小的|T⟩与|S⟩输出差异。提出的新型部分容错方案在保持高保真度的同时显著降低了资源成本。开发的预筛选和采样技术大幅提升了非Clifford电路的模拟效率,为评估和优化量子纠错方案提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Magic state cultivation prepares high-fidelity magic states at low expected space-time costs; however, the exact performance of some schemes is unsettled due to the difficulty in simulating non-Clifford circuits. Here, we use matrix-product states (MPS) based methods to compute the exact performance of two types of fold-transversal cultivation schemes: (i) the Sahay et al method based on the regular surface code S gate, and (ii) a method we propose based on a partially fault-tolerant fold-transversal S gate. We show that for the former protocol at $d=5$, the $|T\rangle$ output reaches similar logical error rates to the $|S\rangle$ output, traditionally used as a cheap full Clifford proxy. This contrasts with the $\sim10\times$ discrepancy reported for the $d=5$ colour-code scheme of Gidney et al. We also find that our new $d=5$ scheme has $\sim1.3\times$ lower expected space-time cost while still reaching $10^{-9}$ logical error rate. We show that MPS and Clifford-augmented MPS (CAMPS) perform on par with or even better than the recently introduced near-Clifford simulator Clifft on the hardest $d=5$ regular surface code scheme. Additionally, to speed up simulation, we propose a new pre-screening method based on simple Pauli propagation, lowering by up to three orders of magnitude the required number of exact simulations, and use several simulator-agnostic sampling methods such as subset sampling.

</details>
